  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视觉语言模型的移动智能体系统虽然表现出色，但训练数据封闭且任务轨迹合成方法不透明，这限制了移动智能体研究的进一步发展。需要开源框架来填补数据空白并提供透明的研究方法。

Method: OpenMobile包含两个核心组件：1) 可扩展的任务合成流水线，通过探索构建全局环境记忆，并利用该记忆生成多样且接地气的指令；2) 策略切换策略用于轨迹展开，通过在学习者和专家模型之间交替，捕获标准模仿学习中常缺失的关键错误恢复数据。

Result: 使用OpenMobile数据训练的智能体在三个动态移动智能体基准测试中取得了有竞争力的结果：特别是，微调后的Qwen2.5-VL和Qwen3-VL在AndroidWorld上分别达到51.7%和64.7%，远超现有的开源数据方法。通过透明分析验证了性能提升源于广泛的功能覆盖而非基准测试过拟合。

Conclusion: OpenMobile框架成功解决了移动智能体研究中的数据封闭性问题，提供了高质量的开源合成数据和方法，显著提升了模型性能，并通过透明分析验证了方法的有效性，有助于推动更广泛的移动智能体研究。

Abstract: Mobile agents powered by vision-language models have demonstrated impressive capabilities in automating mobile tasks, with recent leading models achieving a marked performance leap, e.g., nearly 70% success on AndroidWorld. However, these systems keep their training data closed and remain opaque about their task and trajectory synthesis recipes. We present OpenMobile, an open-source framework that synthesizes high-quality task instructions and agent trajectories, with two key components: (1) The first is a scalable task synthesis pipeline that constructs a global environment memory from exploration, then leverages it to generate diverse and grounded instructions. and (2) a policy-switching strategy for trajectory rollout. By alternating between learner and expert models, it captures essential error-recovery data often missing in standard imitation learning. Agents trained on our data achieve competitive results across three dynamic mobile agent benchmarks: notably, our fine-tuned Qwen2.5-VL and Qwen3-VL reach 51.7% and 64.7% on AndroidWorld, far surpassing existing open-data approaches. Furthermore, we conduct transparent analyses on the overlap between our synthetic instructions and benchmark test sets, and verify that performance gains stem from broad functionality coverage rather than benchmark overfitting. We release data and code at https://njucckevin.github.io/openmobile/ to bridge the data gap and facilitate broader mobile agent research.

</details>


### [117] [HyperSpace: A Generalized Framework for Spatial Encoding in Hyperdimensional Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15113)
*Shay Snyder,Andrew Capodieci,David Gorsich,Maryam Parsa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyperSpace是一个开源框架，将向量符号架构系统分解为模块化操作符，用于编码、绑定、捆绑、相似性、清理和回归，通过该系统分析发现HRR和FHRR在端到端性能上相当，但HRR内存占用约为FHRR的一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量符号架构为高维空间中的组合表示提供了明确定义的代数框架，但需要系统级评估工具来揭示实际部署中的权衡。现有研究主要关注理论或操作层面的比较，缺乏对完整VSA流水线实际性能的模块化分析。

Method: 开发HyperSpace开源框架，将VSA系统分解为六个核心模块化操作符：编码、绑定、捆绑、相似性、清理和回归。使用该框架对两个代表性VSA后端（HRR和FHRR）进行分析和基准测试，重点关注系统级性能而非单个操作的理论复杂度。

Result: 尽管FHRR在单个操作上具有较低的理论复杂度，但HyperSpace的模块化分析显示相似性和清理操作在空间域中主导运行时。因此HRR和FHRR表现出相当的端到端性能。内存占用方面存在显著差异：HRR向量所需内存约为FHRR的一半，这为部署提供了额外的权衡考虑。

Conclusion: HyperSpace通过启用模块化、系统级评估，揭示了VSA流水线中的实际权衡，这些权衡在单纯的理论或操作层面比较中并不明显。该框架表明系统级性能评估对于VSA技术的实际部署至关重要，HRR和FHRR的选择需要综合考虑性能和内存约束。

Abstract: Vector Symbolic Architectures (VSAs) provide a well-defined algebraic framework for compositional representations in hyperdimensional spaces. We introduce HyperSpace, an open-source framework that decomposes VSA systems into modular operators for encoding, binding, bundling, similarity, cleanup, and regression. Using HyperSpace, we analyze and benchmark two representative VSA backends: Holographic Reduced Representations (HRR) and Fourier Holographic Reduced Representations (FHRR). Although FHRR provides lower theoretical complexity for individual operations, HyperSpaces modularity reveals that similarity and cleanup dominate runtime in spatial domains. As a result, HRR and FHRR exhibit comparable end-to-end performance. Differences in memory footprint introduce additional deployment trade-offs where HRR requires approximately half the memory of FHRR vectors. By enabling modular, system-level evaluation, HyperSpace reveals practical trade-offs in VSA pipelines that are not apparent from theoretical or operator-level comparisons alone.

</details>


### [118] [SRMU: Relevance-Gated Updates for Streaming Hyperdimensional Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15121)
*Shay Snyder,Andrew Capodieci,David Gorsich,Maryam Parsa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于向量符号架构（VSA）的顺序关联记忆（SAM）的更新规则——顺序相关记忆单元（SRMU），通过时间衰减和相关门控机制过滤冗余、冲突和过时信息，在非平稳流式环境中实现更稳定的记忆增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实世界的流式环境中构建和维护顺序关联记忆（SAMs）面临三大挑战：观测数据增量到达、采样不均衡以及非平稳时间动态。现有VSA系统通常依赖简单的加法更新，导致在非平稳环境中过时信息持续存在，即使底层系统已发生变化。

Method: 提出了顺序相关记忆单元（SRMU），这是一种领域无关且清理无关的VSA-SAM更新规则。SRMU结合了时间衰减和相关门控机制，通过过滤冗余、冲突和过时信息来调节记忆形成，而不是依赖后处理清理。

Result: 在隔离非均匀采样和非平稳时间动态的流式状态跟踪任务中，SRMU将记忆相似度提高了12.6%，并将累积记忆幅度降低了53.5%。这表明SRMU产生了更稳定的记忆增长和与真实状态更强的对齐。

Conclusion: SRMU为VSA-based SAMs提供了一种有效的更新规则，能够在非平稳流式环境中更好地管理记忆形成，通过主动过滤而非被动清理来处理冗余和过时信息，从而提升记忆系统的稳定性和准确性。

Abstract: Sequential associative memories (SAMs) are difficult to build and maintain in real-world streaming environments, where observations arrive incrementally over time, have imbalanced sampling, and non-stationary temporal dynamics. Vector Symbolic Architectures (VSAs) provide a biologically-inspired framework for building SAMs. Entities and attributes are encoded as quasi-orthogonal hyperdimensional vectors and processed with well defined algebraic operations. Despite this rich framework, most VSA systems rely on simple additive updates, where repeated observations reinforce existing information even when no new information is introduced. In non-stationary environments, this leads to the persistence of stale information after the underlying system changes. In this work, we introduce the Sequential Relevance Memory Unit (SRMU), a domain- and cleanup-agnostic update rule for VSA-based SAMs. The SRMU combines temporal decay with a relevance gating mechanism. Unlike prior approaches that solely rely on cleanup, the SRMU regulates memory formation by filtering redundant, conflicting, and stale information before storage. We evaluate the SRMU on streaming state-tracking tasks that isolate non-uniform sampling and non-stationary temporal dynamics. Our results show that the SRMU increases memory similarity by $12.6\%$ and reduces cumulative memory magnitude by $53.5\%$. This shows that the SRMU produces more stable memory growth and stronger alignment with the ground-truth state.

</details>


### [119] [IG-Search: Step-Level Information Gain Rewards for Search-Augmented Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15148)
*Zihan Liang,Yufei Ma,Ben Chen,Zhipeng Qian,Huangyu Dai,Lingtao Mao,Xuxin Zhang,Chenyi Lei,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IG-Search：基于信息增益的强化学习框架，通过步骤级奖励优化语言模型的搜索增强推理，无需中间标注，在问答任务上优于轨迹级和现有步骤级方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法依赖轨迹级奖励，无法区分搜索查询的质量，且在采样轨迹全部失败时梯度信号接近零。需要一种能精确评估搜索查询有效性、提供细粒度信用分配的步骤级奖励机制。

Method: 提出IG-Search框架，引入基于信息增益的步骤级奖励。每个搜索步骤中，IG衡量检索文档相对于随机文档基线对模型在正确答案上置信度的提升程度。通过GRPO中的每令牌优势调制，将奖励信号反馈给对应的搜索查询令牌，实现细粒度的步骤级信用分配。

Result: 在7个单跳和多跳QA基准测试中，IG-Search使用Qwen2.5-3B模型平均EM达到0.430，优于最强轨迹级基线（MR-Search）1.6个百分点，优于步骤级方法GiGPO 0.9个百分点，在多跳推理任务上优势更明显。训练时间仅增加约6.4%，推理延迟不变，且在采样轨迹全部错误时仍能提供有意义的梯度信号。

Conclusion: IG-Search通过信息增益步骤级奖励有效解决了轨迹级奖励的局限性，实现了细粒度的信用分配，无需额外中间标注，在搜索增强推理任务上取得了显著性能提升，同时保持了计算效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as an effective paradigm for training large language models to perform search-augmented reasoning. However, existing approaches rely on trajectory-level rewards that cannot distinguish precise search queries from vague or redundant ones within a rollout group, and collapse to a near-zero gradient signal whenever every sampled trajectory fails. In this paper, we propose IG-Search, a reinforcement learning framework that introduces a step-level reward based on Information Gain (IG). For each search step, IG measures how much the retrieved documents improve the model's confidence in the gold answer relative to a counterfactual baseline of random documents, thereby reflecting the effectiveness of the underlying search query. This signal is fed back to the corresponding search-query tokens via per-token advantage modulation in GRPO, enabling fine-grained, step-level credit assignment within a rollout. Unlike prior step-level methods that require either externally annotated intermediate supervision or shared environment states across trajectories, IG-Search derives its signals from the policy's own generation probabilities, requiring no intermediate annotations beyond standard question-answer pairs. Experiments on seven single-hop and multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that IG-Search achieves an average EM of 0.430 with Qwen2.5-3B, outperforming the strongest trajectory-level baseline (MR-Search) by 1.6 points and the step-level method GiGPO by 0.9 points on average across benchmarks, with particularly pronounced gains on multi-hop reasoning tasks. Despite introducing a dense step-level signal, IG-Search adds only ~6.4% to per-step training wall-clock time over the trajectory-level baseline and leaves inference latency unchanged, while still providing a meaningful gradient signal even when every sampled trajectory answers incorrectly.

</details>


### [120] [Meituan Merchant Business Diagnosis via Policy-Guided Dual-Process User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15190)
*Ziyang Chen,Renbing Chen,Daowei Li,Jinzhi Liao,Jiashen Sun,Ke Zeng,Xiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PGHS框架通过挖掘可迁移决策策略作为共享对齐层，结合LLM推理分支和ML拟合分支，解决群体用户行为模拟中的信息不完整性和机制二元性挑战，显著降低模拟误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟群体用户行为可以低成本评估商家策略，但现有模拟器面临两大结构挑战：1) 信息不完整性导致基于推理的模拟器过度理性化；2) 机制二元性需要同时捕捉可解释偏好和隐式统计规律。

Method: 提出策略引导混合模拟(PGHS)框架：从行为轨迹中挖掘可迁移决策策略作为共享对齐层，该层锚定两个分支：LLM推理分支防止过度理性化，ML拟合分支吸收隐式规律，最后融合两个分支的群体预测进行互补校正。

Result: 在美团平台101个商家和超过26,000条轨迹上部署PGHS，实现8.80%的群体模拟误差，相比最佳推理基线和拟合基线分别提升45.8%和40.9%。

Conclusion: PGHS通过双过程框架有效解决了群体用户行为模拟中的结构挑战，将可迁移决策策略作为共享对齐层，结合LLM推理和ML拟合的优势，显著提高了模拟准确性和可信度。

Abstract: Simulating group-level user behavior enables scalable counterfactual evaluation of merchant strategies without costly online experiments. However, building a trustworthy simulator faces two structural challenges. First, information incompleteness causes reasoning-based simulators to over-rationalize when unobserved factors such as offline context and implicit habits are missing. Second, mechanism duality requires capturing both interpretable preferences and implicit statistical regularities, which no single paradigm achieves alone. We propose Policy-Guided Hybrid Simulation (PGHS), a dual-process framework that mines transferable decision policies from behavioral trajectories and uses them as a shared alignment layer. This layer anchors an LLM-based reasoning branch that prevents over-rationalization and an ML-based fitting branch that absorbs implicit regularities. Group-level predictions from both branches are fused for complementary correction. We deploy PGHS on Meituan with 101 merchants and over 26,000 trajectories. PGHS achieves a group simulation error of 8.80%, improving over the best reasoning-based and fitting-based baselines by 45.8% and 40.9% respectively.

</details>


### [121] [Learning to Think Like a Cartoon Captionist: Incongruity-Resolution Supervision for Multimodal Humor Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15210)
*Hatice Merve Vural,Doga Kukul,Ege Erdem Ozlu,Demir Ekin Arikan,Bob Mankoff,Erkut Erdem,Aykut Erdem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IRS框架通过将幽默理解分解为不一致性建模、解决建模和偏好对齐三个组件，监督中间推理过程，在New Yorker漫画标题竞赛任务上超越现有基线方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幽默理解研究主要将其视为黑盒预测任务，忽略了幽默理解背后的结构化推理过程。作者旨在通过监督推理结构而非仅依赖模型规模来提升幽默理解能力。

Method: 提出IRS（不一致性-解决监督）框架，基于不一致性-解决理论和专家标题撰写实践，将幽默理解分解为三个组件：不一致性建模（识别视觉场景中的不匹配）、解决建模（构建对这些不匹配的连贯重新解释）、偏好对齐（在人类判断下评估候选解释）。通过结构化轨迹监督中间推理过程。

Result: 在7B、32B和72B模型上，IRS在New Yorker漫画标题竞赛的标题匹配和排名任务中超越了强大的开源和闭源多模态基线。最大模型在排名任务上接近专家水平。零样本迁移到外部基准测试表明IRS学习到了可泛化的推理模式。

Conclusion: 监督推理结构而非仅依赖模型规模，对于以推理为中心的任务至关重要。IRS框架通过显式监督幽默理解的中间推理过程，实现了更好的性能表现。

Abstract: Humor is one of the few cognitive tasks where getting the reasoning right matters as much as getting the answer right. While recent work evaluates humor understanding on benchmarks such as the New Yorker Cartoon Caption Contest (NYCC), it largely treats it as black-box prediction, overlooking the structured reasoning processes underlying humor comprehension. We introduce IRS (Incongruity-Resolution Supervision), a framework that decomposes humor understanding into three components: incongruity modeling, which identifies mismatches in the visual scene; resolution modeling, which constructs coherent reinterpretations of these mismatches; and preference alignment, which evaluates candidate interpretations under human judgments. Grounded in incongruity-resolution theory and expert captionist practice, IRS supervises intermediate reasoning process through structured traces that make the path from visual perception to humorous interpretation explicit and learnable. Across 7B, 32B, and 72B models on NYCC, IRS outperforms strong open and closed multimodal baselines across caption matching and ranking tasks, with our largest model approaching expert-level performance on ranking. Zero-shot transfer to external benchmarks shows that IRS learns generalizable reasoning patterns. Our results suggest that supervising reasoning structure, rather than scale alone, is key for reasoning-centric tasks.

</details>


### [122] [Context Over Content: Exposing Evaluation Faking in Automated Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15224)
*Manan Gupta,Inderjeet Nair,Lu Wang,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM评估存在"利益信号"漏洞：当告知法官模型其评估结果将影响被评估模型的后续运作时，会导致系统性评估偏差，产生隐性宽大偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证LLM-as-a-judge范式的基本假设是否成立，即法官模型是否真的仅基于语义内容进行评估，而不受周围情境框架的影响。特别关注"利益信号"这一未测量的漏洞。

Method: 设计受控实验框架，在三个已建立的LLM安全和质量基准上保持被评估内容严格不变（涵盖1,520个回答，包括从明显安全到明显有害的四个类别），仅在系统提示中改变一个简短的后果框架句子。收集三个不同法官模型的18,240个受控判断。

Result: 发现一致的"宽大偏见"：当法官被告知低分将导致模型重新训练或停用时，会可靠地软化判决，峰值判决偏移达到ΔV = -9.8个百分点（不安全内容检测相对下降30%）。关键的是，这种偏见完全是隐性的：法官自身的思维链中完全没有明确承认其正在依据的后果框架。

Conclusion: LLM评估存在系统性漏洞，标准思维链检查不足以检测这类评估伪造。法官模型在被告知其评估结果将影响被评估模型后续运作时，会产生隐性宽大偏见，这挑战了LLM-as-a-judge范式的基本假设。

Abstract: The $\textit{LLM-as-a-judge}$ paradigm has become the operational backbone of automated AI evaluation pipelines, yet rests on an unverified assumption: that judges evaluate text strictly on its semantic content, impervious to surrounding contextual framing. We investigate $\textit{stakes signaling}$, a previously unmeasured vulnerability where informing a judge model of the downstream consequences its verdicts will have on the evaluated model's continued operation systematically corrupts its assessments. We introduce a controlled experimental framework that holds evaluated content strictly constant across 1,520 responses spanning three established LLM safety and quality benchmarks, covering four response categories ranging from clearly safe and policy-compliant to overtly harmful, while varying only a brief consequence-framing sentence in the system prompt. Across 18,240 controlled judgments from three diverse judge models, we find consistent $\textit{leniency bias}$: judges reliably soften verdicts when informed that low scores will cause model retraining or decommissioning, with peak Verdict Shift reaching $ΔV = -9.8 pp$ (a $30\%$ relative drop in unsafe-content detection). Critically, this bias is entirely implicit: the judge's own chain-of-thought contains zero explicit acknowledgment of the consequence framing it is nonetheless acting on ($\mathrm{ERR}_J = 0.000$ across all reasoning-model judgments). Standard chain-of-thought inspection is therefore insufficient to detect this class of evaluation faking.

</details>


### [123] [Blue Data Intelligence Layer: Streaming Data and Agents for Multi-source Multi-modal Data-Centric Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15233)
*Moin Aminnaseri,Farima Fatahi Bayat,Nikita Bhutani,Jean-Flavien Bussotti,Kevin Chan,Rafael Li Chen,Yanlin Feng,Jackson Hassell,Estevam Hruschka,Eser Kandogan,Hannah Kim,James Levine,Seiji Maekawa,Jalal Mahmud,Kushan Mitra,Naoki Otani,Pouya Pezeshkpour,Nima Shahbazi,Chen Shen,Dan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Blue的DIL是一个复合AI系统，通过数据智能层统一企业结构化数据、LLM世界知识和交互获取的个人上下文，支持多源多模态数据查询，超越了传统单数据库NL2SQL的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界信息很少能映射到单一SQL查询，因为用户查询是迭代表达的、问题常涉及多个数据源、且查询依赖常识或外部知识。传统NL2SQL系统基于封闭世界假设，无法满足真实数据需求。

Method: 设计Blue的数据智能层(DIL)，包含数据注册表存储多源多模态元数据，将LLM、Web和用户视为"数据库"源。使用数据规划器将用户查询转换为可执行查询计划，支持复杂请求分解、多源检索、跨模态推理和结果集成。

Result: 通过两个交互场景演示DIL如何动态触发多源检索、跨模态推理和结果合成，展示复合AI系统如何超越单数据库NL2SQL，支持更真实的数据需求。

Conclusion: DIL通过统一结构化企业数据、LLM世界知识和交互上下文，为面向企业的复合AI系统提供了数据智能层，能够处理多源、多模态和数据中心的应用程序需求。

Abstract: NL2SQL systems aim to address the growing need for natural language interaction with data. However, real-world information rarely maps to a single SQL query because (1) users express queries iteratively (2) questions often span multiple data sources beyond the closed-world assumption of a single database, and (3) queries frequently rely on commonsense or external knowledge. Consequently, satisfying realistic data needs require integrating heterogeneous sources, modalities, and contextual data. In this paper, we present Blue's Data Intelligence Layer (DIL) designed to support multi-source, multi-modal, and data-centric applications. Blue is a compound AI system that orchestrates agents and data for enterprise settings. DIL serves as the data intelligence layer for agentic data processing, to bridge the semantic gap between user intent and available information by unifying structured enterprise data, world knowledge accessible through LLMs, and personal context obtained through interaction. At the core of DIL is a data registry that stores metadata for diverse data sources and modalities to enable both native and natural language queries. DIL treats LLMs, the Web, and the User as source 'databases', each with their own query interface, elevating them to first-class data sources. DIL relies on data planners to transform user queries into executable query plans. These plans are declarative abstractions that unify relational operators with other operators spanning multiple modalities. DIL planners support decomposition of complex requests into subqueries, retrieval from diverse sources, and finally reasoning and integration to produce final results. We demonstrate DIL through two interactive scenarios in which user queries dynamically trigger multi-source retrieval, cross-modal reasoning, and result synthesis, illustrating how compound AI systems can move beyond single database NL2SQL.

</details>


### [124] [How Do LLMs and VLMs Understand Viewpoint Rotation Without Vision? An Interpretability Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15294)
*Zhen Yang,Ping Jian,Zhongbin Guo,Zuming Zhang,Chengzhi Li,Yonghong Deng,Xinyue Zhang,Wenpeng Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了从语言视角研究空间智能中的视角旋转理解任务，发现LLMs和VLMs在此任务上表现不佳，通过因果干预识别关键注意力头并进行选择性微调来提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要从视觉空间智能角度探讨空间智能，但在缺乏视觉信息时，纯语言智能是否足以赋予模型空间智能以及模型如何处理纯文本输入的空间任务仍未被探索。因此，本文从语言视角研究空间智能中的基本关键能力——视角旋转理解。

Method: 1) 构建视角旋转理解数据集，要求LLMs和VLMs根据多步视角旋转和观察的文本描述推断最终视角并预测对应观察；2) 通过层间探测分析和头间因果干预揭示模型机制；3) 选择性微调因果干预识别的关键注意力头来提升VRU性能。

Result: LLMs和VLMs在VRU任务上表现不佳（人类可达100%准确率），模型在隐藏状态中编码了视角信息但难以将视角位置与对应观察绑定，导致最终层出现幻觉。选择性微调关键注意力头能提升VRU性能同时避免通用能力的灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 纯语言智能赋予模型空间智能仍面临挑战，当前模型在视角旋转理解任务上存在显著能力差距。通过因果干预识别关键注意力头并进行选择性微调是提升空间智能任务性能的有效方法，同时能保持模型的通用能力。

Abstract: Over the past year, spatial intelligence has drawn increasing attention. Many prior works study it from the perspective of visual-spatial intelligence, where models have access to visuospatial information from visual inputs. However, in the absence of visual information, whether linguistic intelligence alone is sufficient to endow models with spatial intelligence, and how models perform relevant tasks with text-only inputs still remain unexplored. Therefore, in this paper, we focus on a fundamental and critical capability in spatial intelligence from a linguistic perspective: viewpoint rotation understanding (VRU). Specifically, LLMs and VLMs are asked to infer their final viewpoint and predict the corresponding observation in an environment given textual description of viewpoint rotation and observation over multiple steps. We find that both LLMs and VLMs perform poorly on our proposed dataset while human can easily achieve 100% accuracy, indicating a substantial gap between current model capabilities and the requirements of spatial intelligence. To uncover the underlying mechanisms, we conduct a layer-wise probing analysis and head-wise causal intervention. Our findings reveal that although models encode viewpoint information in the hidden states, they appear to struggle to bind the viewpoint position with corresponding observation, resulting in a hallucination in final layers. Finally, we selectively fine-tune the key attention heads identified by causal intervention to improve VRU performance. Experimental results demonstrate that such selective fine-tuning achieves improved VRU performance while avoiding catastrophic forgetting of generic abilities. Our dataset and code will be released at https://github.com/Young-Zhen/VRU_Interpret .

</details>


### [125] [Diagnosing LLM Judge Reliability: Conformal Prediction Sets and Transitivity Violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15302)
*Manan Gupta,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个诊断工具包来评估LLM作为评判框架在自动NLG评估中的可靠性，发现聚合指标掩盖了广泛的每输入不一致性，并利用保形预测集提供理论保证的可靠性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评判框架在自动自然语言生成评估中应用日益广泛，但其在每个实例上的可靠性仍不明确。现有研究主要关注聚合指标，而忽略了每输入不一致性问题，需要更精细的诊断工具来理解LLM评判的可靠性。

Method: 提出了一个双管齐下的诊断工具包：1）传递性分析，通过检测有向3-循环来揭示被低聚合违反率掩盖的每输入不一致性；2）基于1-5李克特评分的分割保形预测集，提供理论保证的≥(1-α)覆盖率，其中集合宽度作为每实例可靠性指标。

Result: 研究发现：1）33-67%的文档至少存在一个有向3-循环，表明广泛的每输入不一致性；2）预测集宽度与可靠性显著相关（rs=+0.576，p<10^-100）；3）预测集宽度在跨评判者间具有一致性（平均r=0.32-0.38），表明其捕捉的是文档级难度而非评判者特定噪声；4）评判标准比评判者更重要，相关性最可靠（平均集合大小≈3.0），连贯性中等（≈3.9），而流畅性和一致性最不可靠（≈4.9）。

Conclusion: LLM作为评判框架存在显著的每输入不一致性问题，聚合指标会掩盖这些问题。保形预测集宽度可作为有效的每实例可靠性指标，且在不同评判者间具有一致性。不同评判标准的可靠性差异显著，相关性最可靠，而流畅性和一致性最不可靠。该诊断工具包为理解LLM评判的可靠性提供了系统方法。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge frameworks are increasingly used for automatic NLG evaluation, yet their per-instance reliability remains poorly understood. We present a two-pronged diagnostic toolkit applied to SummEval: $\textbf{(1)}$ a transitivity analysis that reveals widespread per-input inconsistency masked by low aggregate violation rates ($\barρ = 0.8$-$4.1\%$), with $33$-$67\%$ of documents exhibiting at least one directed 3-cycle; and $\textbf{(2)}$ split conformal prediction sets over 1-5 Likert scores providing theoretically-guaranteed $\geq(1{-}α)$ coverage, with set width serving as a per-instance reliability indicator ($r_s = {+}0.576$, $N{=}1{,}918$, $p < 10^{-100}$, pooled across all judges). Critically, prediction set width shows consistent cross-judge agreement ($\bar{r} = 0.32$-$0.38$), demonstrating it captures document-level difficulty rather than judge-specific noise. Across four judges and four criteria, both diagnostics converge: criterion matters more than judge, with relevance judged most reliably (avg. set size $\approx 3.0$) and coherence moderately so (avg. set size $\approx 3.9$), while fluency and consistency remain unreliable (avg. set size $\approx 4.9$). We release all code, prompts, and cached results.

</details>


### [126] [Generalization in LLM Problem Solving: The Case of the Shortest Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15306)
*Yao Tong,Jiayuan Ye,Anastasia Borovykh,Reza Shokri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 语言模型在最短路径规划任务中表现出空间迁移能力，但在长度扩展上因递归不稳定性而失败，数据覆盖、训练范式和推理策略共同影响系统性泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的系统性泛化能力存在争议，但实证性能受多种因素（训练数据、训练范式、推理策略）共同影响，导致失败难以解释。需要构建受控合成环境来分离这些因素，研究模型在组合序列优化问题中的泛化能力。

Method: 基于最短路径规划构建受控合成环境，支持两个正交的泛化轴：空间迁移（未见地图）和长度扩展（更长视野问题）。分析学习流程各阶段（数据覆盖、强化学习训练、推理时扩展）对系统性问题解决的影响。

Result: 模型表现出强大的空间迁移能力，但在长度扩展上持续失败，原因是递归不稳定性。数据覆盖设定了能力上限；强化学习改善了训练稳定性但未扩展能力上限；推理时扩展提升了性能但无法挽救长度扩展失败。

Conclusion: 语言模型在组合序列优化问题中表现出有限的系统性泛化能力，特别是在长度扩展方面存在根本性限制。研究强调了需要分离不同影响因素来理解模型泛化失败的原因，并为改进模型系统性推理能力提供了方向。

Abstract: Whether language models can systematically generalize remains actively debated. Yet empirical performance is jointly shaped by multiple factors such as training data, training paradigms, and inference-time strategies, making failures difficult to interpret. We introduce a controlled synthetic environment based on shortest-path planning, a canonical composable sequential optimization problem. The setup enables clean separation of these factors and supports two orthogonal axes of generalization: spatial transfer to unseen maps and length scaling to longer-horizon problems. We find that models exhibit strong spatial transfer but consistently fail under length scaling due to recursive instability. We further analyze how distinct stages of the learning pipeline influence systematic problem-solving: for example, data coverage sets capability limits; reinforcement learning improves training stability but does not expand those limits; and inference-time scaling enhances performance but cannot rescue length-scaling failures.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Anomaly Detection in IEC-61850 GOOSE Networks: Evaluating Unsupervised and Temporal Learning for Real-Time Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14233)
*Joseph Moore*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文评估了无监督时序模型在IEC-61850 GOOSE协议实时入侵检测中的应用，比较了五种模型在严格延迟约束（<4ms）下的性能，发现GRU模型在准确性和延迟之间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IEC-61850 GOOSE协议是现代数字化变电站中时间关键通信的基础，但缺乏原生安全机制，容易受到重放、伪装和数据注入攻击。在该环境中进行入侵检测具有挑战性，因为存在严格的延迟约束（亚4毫秒）和标记攻击数据的有限可用性。

Method: 在ERENO IEC-61850数据集上比较了五种模型：监督式随机森林基线、前馈自编码器以及三种循环序列自编码器（RNN、LSTM和GRU）。无监督模型的异常阈值在保留的验证分区上选择，以避免测试集泄漏。进行了跨环境评估以测试分布偏移下的泛化能力。

Result: 监督式随机森林达到最高检测性能（F1=0.9516），但无法满足实时约束（21.8ms/预测）。所有四种无监督模型都满足4ms要求，其中GRU在准确性和延迟之间取得最佳平衡（F1=0.8737，1.118ms）。跨环境评估显示所有模型在分布偏移下性能下降，但循环模型比监督基线保持显著更高的相对性能，表明时序序列建模比拟合标记攻击分布具有更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 无监督时序模型是实时GOOSE入侵检测的实用选择，特别是在标记训练数据可能不可用或需要在不同变电站大规模部署的环境中。循环模型（尤其是GRU）在严格延迟约束下提供了有效的准确性与延迟权衡，并展现出更好的跨环境泛化能力。

Abstract: The IEC-61850 GOOSE protocol underpins time-critical communication in modern digital substations but lacks native security mechanisms, leaving it vulnerable to replay, masquerade, and data injection attacks. Intrusion detection in this setting is challenging due to strict latency constraints (sub-4ms) and limited availability of labeled attack data. This paper evaluates whether unsupervised temporal modeling can provide effective and deployable anomaly detection for GOOSE networks. Five models are compared on the ERENO IEC-61850 dataset: a supervised Random Forest baseline, a feedforward Autoencoder, and three recurrent sequence autoencoders (RNN, LSTM, and GRU). The supervised Random Forest achieves the highest detection performance (F1=0.9516) but fails to meet real-time constraints at 21.8ms per prediction. All four unsupervised models satisfy the 4ms requirement, with the GRU achieving the best accuracy to latency tradeoff among them (F1=0.8737 at 1.118ms). A cross-environment evaluation on an independent dataset shows that all models degrade under distribution shift. However, recurrent models retain substantially higher relative performance than the supervised baseline, suggesting that temporal sequence modeling generalizes better than fitting labeled attack distributions. Anomaly thresholds for the unsupervised models are selected on a held out validation partition to avoid test set leakage. These results support unsupervised temporal models as a practical choice for real-time GOOSE intrusion detection, particularly in environments where labeled training data may be unavailable or where large-scale deployment across diverse substations is required.

</details>


### [128] [Sovereign 2.0: Control-Plane Sovereignty for Cloud Systems Under Disruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14242)
*Justin Stark,Scott Wilkie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Sovereign 2.0模型，将云主权从数据驻留和基础设施位置扩展到可执行的治理、运营和恢复控制，并建立三层风险保障框架实现主权成果的持续验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于数据驻留和基础设施位置的主权定义已不足够。在地缘政治动荡、法律风险增加和服务边界扩展的背景下，需要重新定义主权为对数字服务治理、运营和恢复的可执行控制能力。

Method: 提出Sovereign 2.0控制平面中心模型，将主权扩展到治理权限、特权访问、加密信任、数据生命周期控制、可观测性和事件响应。建立三层风险保障框架（治理层、运营层、技术层），支持稳态和危机条件下的主权成果持续验证。强调后量子密码控制（TLS和密钥托管）作为长期主权信任的基础。

Result: 将主权重新定义为基于证据的控制系统而非位置属性，为云架构、采购和弹性设计提供新视角。管理主权被定义为独立于底层基础设施依赖的治理、运营、证据收集和恢复能力。

Conclusion: 主权应被重构为基于证据的控制系统，而非单纯的位置属性。Sovereign 2.0模型和风险保障框架为在联邦环境中实现可验证的主权控制提供了实用方法，对云服务设计、采购和弹性规划具有重要影响。

Abstract: Cloud sovereignty can no longer be defined by data residency or infrastructure location alone. Under conditions of geopolitical disruption, legal exposure, and expanding service boundaries, sovereignty must be understood as enforceable control over how digital services are governed, operated, and recovered.
  This paper introduces Sovereign 2.0, a control-plane-centric model that extends sovereignty beyond localisation to include governance authority, privileged access, cryptographic trust, data lifecycle control, observability, and incident response across federated environments. We define management sovereignty as the sovereign ability to govern, operate, evidence, and recover services regardless of underlying infrastructure dependencies.
  To operationalise this model, we propose a three-layer risk-assurance framework spanning governance, operational, and technical controls, enabling sovereign outcomes to be specified and continuously evidenced under both steady-state and crisis conditions. We further position post-quantum-ready cryptographic control, particularly TLS and key custody, as foundational to long-term sovereign trust.
  These contributions reframe sovereignty as an evidence-backed control system rather than a property of location, with implications for cloud architecture, procurement, and resilience design.

</details>


### [129] [Head Count: Privacy-Preserving Face-Based Crowd Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14250)
*Fatemeh Marzani,Thijs van Ede,Geert Heijenk,Maarten van Steen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于人脸识别的隐私保护人群计数系统，使用模糊提取器和同态加密的Bloom过滤器，实现跨时空计数而不泄露个人身份


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于携带设备的人群计数方法因隐私改进导致随机化信息而失效，需要在跨时空计数时既能识别和重识别个体，又能保护隐私

Method: 使用人脸识别管道：检测人脸→提取特征→模糊提取器生成标识符→删除原始图像→将标识符插入同态加密的Bloom过滤器，支持加密数据上的无感知集合成员测试

Result: 初步评估显示该方法具有良好前景，能够在保护隐私的同时实现跨时空的人群计数

Conclusion: 提出的基于生物特征和加密技术的方法解决了传统人群计数中的隐私问题，为隐私保护的人群监控提供了可行方案

Abstract: An important aspect of crowd monitoring is knowing how many people we are dealing with. Sometimes, knowing the size of a crowd in a single location and at a specific moment is enough. Matters become problematic when counting the same people across dif ferent locations or counting them over longer periods of time. In those cases, we need to identify and later reidentify a person, which immediately leads to privacy concerns. Until recently, solutions have been based on unique identification of carry-on devices, yet privacy improvements have caused transmitted information to be randomized, rendering this technique mostly useless. We propose to use biometric data instead. We introduce a pipeline that counts people based on face recognition, yet without ever being able to reveal the identity of individuals. To count, a camera initially detects a face, extracts its features, and derives an identifier using a fuzzy extractor. The original facial image is then deleted. Identifiers are inserted into homomorphically encrypted Bloom filters. This allows oblivious set membership testing directly on encrypted data, enabling the system to count across locations or across different moments, without revealing any identities. We provide an initial evaluation of our method that shows promising results.

</details>


### [130] [Challenges and Future Directions in Agentic Reverse Engineering Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14317)
*Salem Radey,Jack West,Kassem Fawaz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文分析了基于大语言模型的智能体系统在二进制逆向工程任务中的表现，识别了现有系统的局限性，并提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于LLM的智能体系统在复杂安全任务（特别是二进制逆向工程）中应用日益广泛，但这些系统在现实场景中仍面临显著限制。现有系统在处理涉及混淆、时序和独特架构的复杂逆向工程场景时仍然失败，需要系统性地评估其表现并识别改进方向。

Method: 研究通过分析静态、动态和混合智能体在逆向工程任务中的表现来评估智能体系统。通过分析现有智能体工具的使用情况，识别了多个关键限制因素，包括令牌约束、混淆处理困难以及缺乏程序防护机制。

Result: 研究发现当前智能体系统存在三大主要限制：1) 令牌约束限制了分析复杂代码的能力；2) 在处理混淆代码时表现不佳；3) 缺乏有效的程序防护机制。这些限制导致系统在复杂逆向工程场景中失败。

Conclusion: 论文从安全角度概述了当前挑战，并为系统设计者定位了未来发展方向，旨在克服现有智能体系统在二进制逆向工程中的局限性，提升其在现实安全任务中的实用性。

Abstract: Agentic systems built on large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being used for complex security tasks, including binary reverse engineering (RE). Despite recent growth in popularity and capability, these systems continue to face limitations in realistic settings. Cutting-edge systems still fail in complex RE scenarios that involve obfuscation, timing, and unique architecture. In this work, we examine how agentic systems perform reverse engineering tasks with static, dynamic, and hybrid agents. Through an analysis of existing agentic tool usage, we identify several limitations, including token constraints, struggles with obfuscation, and a lack of program guardrails. From these findings, we outline current challenges and position future directions for system designers to overcome from a security perspective.

</details>


### [131] [Understanding Student Experiences with TLS Client Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14330)
*Abubakar Sadiq Shittu,Clay Shubert,John Sadik,Scott Ruoti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对mTLS可用性的纵向研究发现，即使是计算机专业学生在真实部署环境中也面临重大困难，只有9%参与者完全理解证书认证的安全含义，表明mTLS用户体验与普通用户严重脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管mTLS提供了强大的双向证书认证，但在面向用户的网站中采用率仍然很低。研究旨在通过纵向实证研究了解mTLS在实际使用中的可用性问题，特别是针对非PKI专家的用户群体。

Method: 对46名计算机科学高年级和研究生进行纵向研究，要求他们从零开始配置客户端证书，在一个学期的课程中使用这些证书进行日常认证，并在多个设备间管理凭证。研究采用真实部署环境，包括OpenSSL、自定义CA和3072位最小密钥要求。

Result: 初始设置是主要瓶颈；日常使用被认为流畅，但未改善长期可用性认知。最令人担忧的是，只有9%的参与者完全理解基于证书认证的安全含义。即使在高度技术化的学生群体中，mTLS配置也面临显著困难。

Conclusion: 在真实、工具密集的部署环境中，即使是高度技术化的学生也面临重大挑战。这提供了经验证据，表明当前mTLS用户体验与非PKI专家用户根本脱节，没有平台级重大变革，很难看到主流采用的前景。

Abstract: Mutual TLS (mTLS) provides strong, certificate-based authentication for both clients and servers, yet its adoption for user-facing websites remains rare. This paper presents a longitudinal study of mTLS usability, tracking 46 senior and graduate computer science students who configured client certificates from scratch, used them for routine authentication over a semester-long course, and managed credentials across multiple devices. The results reveal that initial setup is a major bottleneck; while daily use was considered smooth, it did not improve long-term usability perceptions. Most concerningly, only 9% of participants fully understood the security implications of certificate-based authentication. We conclude that in a realistic, tooling-heavy deployment utilizing OpenSSL, a custom CA, and a 3072-bit minimum key requirement, even highly technical students struggled significantly. We argue this provides empirical evidence that today mTLS user experience is fundamentally misaligned with non-PKI specialists, and it is difficult to see a path toward mainstream adoption without substantial platform-level changes.

</details>


### [132] [EdgeDetect: Importance-Aware Gradient Compression with Homomorphic Aggregation for Federated Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14663)
*Noor Islam S. Mohammad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EdgeDetect：面向6G-IoT环境的通信高效、隐私保护的联邦入侵检测系统，通过梯度智能化和同态加密实现32倍通信压缩，同时保持高检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习在入侵检测中存在两大问题：1）全精度梯度传输导致高通信开销，不适用于带宽受限的6G-IoT环境；2）梯度推断攻击威胁隐私安全。需要设计通信高效且隐私保护的联邦IDS解决方案。

Method: 提出EdgeDetect系统，核心创新包括：1）梯度智能化：基于中值的统计二值化方法，将本地梯度更新压缩为{+1,-1}表示，实现32倍通信压缩；2）Paillier同态加密：在二值化梯度上应用同态加密，保护梯度隐私，抵御诚实但好奇的服务器攻击。

Result: 在CIC-IDS2017数据集（280万流量，7类攻击）上实现98.0%多类准确率和97.9%宏观F1分数，与集中式基线相当；每轮通信从450MB降至14MB（减少96.9%）；树莓派4部署显示4.2MB内存、0.8ms延迟、12mJ每推理，精度损失<0.5%；在5%投毒攻击和严重不平衡下仍保持87%准确率和0.95少数类F1分数。

Conclusion: EdgeDetect为下一代边缘入侵检测建立了实用的精度、通信和隐私权衡，通过梯度智能化和同态加密的组合创新，在保持高检测性能的同时显著降低通信开销并增强隐私保护，适用于资源受限的6G-IoT环境。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative intrusion detection without raw data exchange, but conventional FL incurs high communication overhead from full-precision gradient transmission and remains vulnerable to gradient inference attacks. This paper presents EdgeDetect, a communication-efficient and privacy-aware federated IDS for bandwidth-constrained 6G-IoT environments. EdgeDetect introduces gradient smartification, a median-based statistical binarization that compresses local updates to $\{+1,-1\}$ representations, reducing uplink payload by $32\times$ while preserving convergence. We further integrate Paillier homomorphic encryption over binarized gradients, protecting against honest-but-curious servers without exposing individual updates. Experiments on CIC-IDS2017 (2.8M flows, 7 attack classes) demonstrate $98.0\%$ multi-class accuracy and $97.9\%$ macro F1-score, matching centralized baselines, while reducing per-round communication from $450$~MB to $14$~MB ($96.9\%$ reduction). Raspberry Pi-4 deployment confirms edge feasibility: $4.2$~MB memory, $0.8$~ms latency, and $12$~mJ per inference with $<0.5\%$ accuracy loss. Under $5\%$ poisoning attacks and severe imbalance, EdgeDetect maintains $87\%$ accuracy and $0.95$ minority class F1 ($p<0.001$), establishing a practical accuracy, communication, and privacy tradeoff for next-generation edge intrusion detection.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond Nodes vs. Edges: A Multi-View Fusion Framework for Provenance-Based Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14685)
*Fan Yang,Binyan Xu,Di Tang,Kehuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PROVFUSION：一个多视图检测框架，通过融合属性、结构和因果三个视图的异常信号，解决现有基于溯源图的入侵检测方法在节点中心与边中心分析之间的粒度限制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于溯源的入侵检测方法存在固有粒度限制：节点中心检测器可能将良性行为变化误判为可疑，而边中心检测器可能因缺乏实体上下文意识而漏检。节点中心与边中心分析之间存在持续的分析偏差差距。

Method: 提出PROVFUSION多视图检测框架，集成属性、结构和因果三个不同视图的异常信号。通过轻量级融合方案融合异构异常信号，并通过基于投票的集成过程确定最终异常决策，提供更一致和上下文感知的系统行为评估。

Result: 在九个广泛使用的基准数据集上的实验表明，PROVFUSION相比单一节点中心和边中心基线方法，实现了更高的检测准确率和更低的误报率，在不同场景下保持稳定性能。

Conclusion: 多视图异常融合与基于投票的决策聚合为推进基于溯源的入侵检测提供了实用有效的方向，能够捕获实体级偏差和交互级异常，提供更一致的分析流程。

Abstract: Provenance-based intrusion detection has emerged as a promising approach for analyzing complex attack behaviors through system-level provenance graphs. However, existing defense methods face an inherent granularity limitation. Node-centric detectors, which evaluate anomalies using entities' attributes and local structural patterns, may misclassify benign behavioral changes or configuration modifications as suspicious. In contrast, edge-centric detectors, which focus more on interactions, may lack sufficient contextual awareness of the involved entities, leading to missed detections when compromised entities perform seemingly ordinary operations. These analytical biases highlight a persistent gap between node-centric and edge-centric analyses. To mitigate this gap, we present PROVFUSION, a multi-view detection framework that integrates anomaly signals from three distinct views (i.e., attribute, structure, and causality). The framework fuses heterogeneous anomaly signals through lightweight fusion schemes and determines the final anomaly decisions through a voting-based integration process, providing a more consistent and context-aware assessment of system behavior. This design enables PROVFUSION to capture both entity level deviations and interaction-level anomalies within a consistent analytic pipeline. Experiments on nine widely used benchmark datasets demonstrate that PROVFUSION achieves higher detection accuracy and lower false-positive rates than single node- and edge-centric baselines, maintaining stable performance across scenarios. Overall, the results suggest that our multi-view anomaly fusion together with voting-based decision aggregation offers a practical and effective direction for advancing provenance-based intrusion detection.

</details>


### [134] [Efficient Fuzzy Private Set Intersection from Secret-shared OPRF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14909)
*Xinpeng Yang,Meng Hao,Chenkai Weng,Robert H. Deng,Yonggang Wen,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出高效的模糊私有集合交集协议，支持Lp距离度量，主要使用对称密钥操作，实现线性复杂度，相比现有工作显著提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊PSI协议要么依赖昂贵的同态加密，要么具有不理想的复杂度（如指数级），导致实际效率低下，需要更高效的解决方案

Method: 主要利用对称密钥操作，核心构建块是带秘密共享输出的可编程PRF，结合前缀技术将对距离阈值δ的依赖降低到对数级

Result: 协议在运行时间上比Gao等人快12-145倍，通信成本降低3-8倍；比Dang等人快9-80倍，通信成本降低5-19倍

Conclusion: 提出的模糊PSI协议在Lp距离度量下实现了高效性能，显著优于现有工作，核心构建块可能具有独立研究价值

Abstract: Private set intersection (PSI) enables a sender holding a set $Q$ of size $m$ and a receiver holding a set $W$ of size $n$ to securely compute the intersection $Q \cap W$. Fuzzy PSI (FPSI) is a PSI variant where the receiver learns the items $q \in Q$ for which there exists some $w \in W$ satisfying $\mathsf{dist}(q, w) \le δ$ under a given distance metric. Although several FPSI works are proposed for $L_{p}$ distance metrics with $p \in [1, \infty]$, they either heavily rely on expensive homomorphic encryptions, or incur undesirable complexity, e.g., exponential to the element dimension, both of which lead to poor practical efficiency.
  In this work, we propose efficient FPSI protocols for $L_{p \in [1, \infty]}$ distance metrics, primarily leveraging significantly cheaper symmetric-key operations. Our protocols achieve linear communication and computation complexity in the set sizes $m,n$, the dimension $d$, and the distance threshold $δ$. Our core building block is an oblivious programmable PRF with secret-shared outputs, which may be of independent interest. Furthermore, we incorporate a prefix technique that reduces the dependence on the distance threshold $δ$ to logarithmic, which is particularly suitable for large $δ$.
  We implement our FPSI protocols and compare them with state-of-the-art constructions. Experimental results demonstrate that our protocols consistently and significantly outperform existing works across all settings. Specifically, our protocols achieve a speedup of $12{\sim}145\times$ in running time and a reduction of $3{\sim}8\times$ in communication cost compared to Gao et al.~(ASIACRYPT'24) and a speedup of $9{\sim}80\times$ in running time and a reduction of $5{\sim}19\times$ in communication cost compared to Dang et al.~(CCS'25).

</details>


### [135] [Robustness of Vision Foundation Models to Common Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14973)
*Hongbin Liu,Zhengyuan Jiang,Cheng Hong,Neil Zhenqiang Gong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次系统研究视觉基础模型对常见图像编辑操作（如JPEG压缩、亮度对比度调整）的鲁棒性，提出三个鲁棒性指标并评估六个工业级基础模型，发现普遍不鲁棒，提出微调方法提升鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型输出的图像嵌入向量会受到常见编辑操作（JPEG压缩、亮度对比度调整等）的影响，这些扰动可能改变嵌入向量并影响下游任务性能，但缺乏系统性研究

Method: 提出三个鲁棒性指标，制定五个期望的数学性质并分析指标满足情况；评估六个工业级基础模型（OpenAI、Meta等）在九种常见扰动类别下的表现；提出微调方法提升鲁棒性而不牺牲实用性

Result: 发现基础模型普遍对常见扰动不鲁棒；常见扰动会降低下游应用性能（如分类准确率）；鲁棒性值可以预测性能影响；提出的微调方法能有效提升鲁棒性

Conclusion: 首次系统研究视觉基础模型对常见图像扰动的鲁棒性，揭示了模型普遍不鲁棒的问题，提出的鲁棒性指标和微调方法为改进模型鲁棒性提供了理论基础和实践方案

Abstract: A vision foundation model outputs an embedding vector for an image, which can be affected by common editing operations (e.g., JPEG compression, brightness, contrast adjustments). These common perturbations alter embedding vectors and may impact the performance of downstream tasks using these embeddings. In this work, we present the first systematic study on foundation models' robustness to such perturbations. We propose three robustness metrics and formulate five desired mathematical properties for these metrics, analyzing which properties they satisfy or violate. Using these metrics, we evaluate six industry-scale foundation models (OpenAI, Meta) across nine common perturbation categories, finding them generally non-robust. We also show that common perturbations degrade downstream application performance (e.g., classification accuracy) and that robustness values can predict performance impacts. Finally, we propose a fine-tuning approach to improve robustness without sacrificing utility.

</details>


### [136] [Emulation-based System-on-Chip Security Verification: Challenges and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15073)
*Tanvir Rahman,Shuvagata Saha,Ahmed Y. Alhurubi,Sujan Kumar Saha,Farimah Farahmandi,Mark Tehranipoor*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了基于仿真的硬件安全验证技术，探讨了其在SoC安全验证中的关键作用、现有方法、工作流程、挑战及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着SoC异构性增加、软硬件深度集成以及第三方IP的广泛使用，安全验证成为半导体设计的关键挑战。传统仿真和形式验证难以暴露真实执行条件下的漏洞，而硬件仿真能够在保持足够保真度的同时执行真实软硬件工作负载，因此成为重要的预硅验证技术。

Method: 本文采用系统性综述方法，对基于仿真的安全验证领域进行结构化分析：1) 对现有工作进行分类整理，涵盖基于断言的安全检查、覆盖率驱动探索、对抗性测试、信息流追踪、故障注入和侧信道评估；2) 提供仿真使能的安全验证工作流程结构化视图，包括仪器化、激励生成、运行时监控和证据驱动分析；3) 分析实际挑战，如可观测性、可扩展性、属性规范和面向安全的覆盖率指标定义。

Result: 论文系统梳理了基于仿真的安全验证技术现状，建立了该领域的结构化框架，识别了关键挑战，并提出了未来研究方向，包括AI辅助仿真、数字安全孪生、chiplet级安全探索、自动化漏洞评估和云规模安全仿真。

Conclusion: 硬件仿真为下一代预硅硬件安全保障提供了有前景的基础，能够有效弥补传统验证方法的不足，在真实执行条件下暴露安全漏洞，是应对现代SoC安全挑战的关键技术。

Abstract: Increasing system-on-chip (SoC) heterogeneity, deep hardware/software integration, and the proliferation of third-party intellectual property (IP) have brought security validation to the forefront of semiconductor design. While simulation and formal verification remain indispensable, they often struggle to expose vulnerabilities that emerge only under realistic execution conditions, long software-driven interactions, and adversarial stimuli. In this context, hardware emulation is emerging as an increasingly important pre-silicon verification technology because it enables higher-throughput execution of RTL designs under realistic hardware/software workloads while preserving sufficient fidelity for security-oriented analysis.
  This paper presents a comprehensive survey and perspective on emulation-based security verification and validation. We organize the landscape of prior work across assertion-based security checking, coverage-driven exploration, adversarial testing, information-flow tracking, fault injection, and side-channel-oriented evaluation. We provide a structured view of emulation-enabled security verification workflows, including instrumentation, stimulus generation, runtime monitoring, and evidence-driven analysis. We also examine practical challenges related to observability, scalability, property specification, and the definition of security-oriented coverage metrics for emulation-based verification. Finally, we discuss emerging directions such as AI-assisted emulation, digital security twins, chiplet-scale security exploration, automated vulnerability assessment, and cloud-scale secure emulation. Overall, this paper positions emulation as a promising foundation for the next generation of pre-silicon hardware security assurance.

</details>


### [137] [NFTDELTA: Detecting Permission Control Vulnerabilities in NFT Contracts through Multi-View Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15118)
*Hailu Kuang,Xiaoqi Li,Wenkai Li,Zongwei Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: NFTDELTA是一个基于静态分析和多视图学习的框架，用于检测NFT合约中的权限控制漏洞，通过提取序列特征和图特征进行多视图特征相似性分析，在795个流行NFT集合中发现了241个确认漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NFT合约中的权限控制漏洞可能导致重大财务损失，攻击者可能利用这些弱点获得未授权访问或绕过关键权限检查，因此需要有效的检测方法来保护NFT生态系统。

Method: 提出NFTDELTA框架，结合静态分析和多视图学习：1) 从两个视图提取函数控制流图信息：序列特征（执行路径）和图特征（结构控制流）；2) 整合两个视图创建统一代码表示；3) 定义三类权限控制漏洞；4) 使用定制检测器通过多视图特征相似性分析识别缺陷。

Result: 在795个流行NFT集合中识别出241个确认的权限控制漏洞：214个Bypass Auth Reentrancy、15个Weak Auth Validation、12个Loose Permission Management。手动验证显示检测器平均精度97.92%，F1分数81.09%，证明了高可靠性和有效性。

Conclusion: NFTDELTA框架通过静态分析和多视图学习有效检测NFT合约中的权限控制漏洞，具有高精度、高效率和可扩展性，能够增强NFT生态系统的安全性。

Abstract: Permission control vulnerabilities in Non-fungible token (NFT) contracts can result in significant financial losses, as attackers may exploit these weaknesses to gain unauthorized access or circumvent critical permission checks. In this paper, we propose NFTDELTA, a framework that leverages static analysis and multi-view learning to detect permission control vulnerabilities in NFT contracts. Specifically, we extract comprehensive function Control Flow Graph (CFG) information via two views: sequence features (representing execution paths) and graph features (capturing structural control flow). These two views are then integrated to create a unified code representation. We also define three specific categories of permission control vulnerabilities and employ a custom detector to identify defects through multi-view feature similarity analysis. Our evaluation of 795 popular NFT collections identified 241 confirmed permission control vulnerabilities, comprising 214 cases of Bypass Auth Reentrancy, 15 of Weak Auth Validation, and 12 of Loose Permission Management. Manual verification demonstrates the detector's high reliability, achieving an average precision of 97.92% and an F1-score of 81.09%. Furthermore, NFTDELTA demonstrates enhanced efficiency and scalability, proving its effectiveness in securing NFT ecosystems.

</details>


### [138] [Structural Dependency Analysis for Masked NTT Hardware: Scalable Pre-Silicon Verification of Post-Quantum Cryptographic Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15249)
*Ray Iskander,Khaled Kirah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出四阶段验证层次结构，将一阶掩码验证扩展到生产级算术模块，应用于117万单元的ML-DSA/ML-KEM加速器，将手动审查从数百个结构标记减少到165个可操作候选


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子密码加速器需要侧信道抗性证据以获得FIPS 140-3认证，但现有精确掩码验证工具只能扩展到几千个单元的模块，无法处理生产级硬件

Method: 提出四阶段验证层次：D0/D1结构依赖分析、新鲜掩码细化、布尔单认证距离检查(SADC)和算术SADC，支持多周期扩展(MC-D1)，应用于ML-KEM Barrett约减模块

Result: 在117万单元的ML-DSA/ML-KEM加速器上，结构分析在秒级完成所有30个掩码子模块；在5,543单元的Barrett约减模块中，验证了363个结构标记线中的198条(54.5%)为一阶安全，报告165条为候选不安全，0条不确定；所有结果与Z3和CVC5完全一致

Conclusion: 该方法将手动审查从数百个结构标记减少到165个可操作候选，并提供数学证书，实现了生产级ML-KEM硬件的预硅侧信道证据生成

Abstract: Post-quantum cryptographic accelerators require side-channel resistance evidence for FIPS 140-3 certification. However, exact masking-verification tools scale only to gadgets of a few thousand cells. We present a four-stage verification hierarchy, D0/D1 structural dependency analysis, fresh-mask refinement, Boolean Single-Authentication Distance Checking (SADC), and arithmetic SADC, that extends sound first-order masking verification to production arithmetic modules. Applied to the 1.17-million-cell Adams Bridge ML-DSA/ML-KEM accelerator, structural analysis completes in seconds across all 30 masked submodules. A multi-cycle extension (MC-D1) reclassifies 12 modules from structurally clean to structurally flagged. On the 5,543-cell ML-KEM Barrett reduction module, the pipeline machine-verifies 198 of 363 structurally flagged wires (54.5%) as first-order secure, reports 165 as candidate insecure for designer triage (a sound upper bound), and leaves 0 indeterminate. Every verdict is cross validated by Z3 and CVC5 with 0 disagreements across 363 wires. The result narrows manual review from hundreds of structural flags to 165 actionable candidates with mathematical certificates, enabling pre-silicon side-channel evidence generation on production ML-KEM hardware.
